Notably, score-based improvement -- providing category-specific scores and justifications -- yields the most significant gains, reducing the error sentence ratio for Appropriateness by up to 33.09%. This work lays the foundation for a more explainable and comprehensive evaluation and improvement of LLM responses to sensitive questions.

</details>


### [154] [VietNormalizer: An Open-Source, Dependency-Free Python Library for Vietnamese Text Normalization in TTS and NLP Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04145)
*Hung Vu Nguyen,Loan Do,Thanh Ngoc Nguyen,Ushik Shrestha Khwakhali,Thanh Pham,Vinh Do,Charlotte Nguyen,Hien Nguyen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: VietNormalizer1 是一个开源、零依赖的 Python 库，用于越南语文本归一化，支持 TTS 和 NLP 应用，通过规则驱动的流水线高效处理多种非标准词。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 越南语文本中存在大量非标准词（如数字、日期、外文等），需转换为可发音形式以支持语音合成和语言处理，但现有工具覆盖不全或依赖复杂。

Method: 采用统一的基于规则的流水线，预编译正则表达式模式，支持整数、小数、日期、时间、货币、百分比、首字母缩略词、外来词音译及 Unicode 标准化等处理。

Result: 实现了高性能、低内存开销的批量文本归一化，无需 GPU 或外部 API，可通过 pip 安装，支持自定义配置并开源发布。

Conclusion: VietNormalizer 填补了越南语文本归一化工具的空白，其规则-based 设计对低资源、声调和黏着性语言具有可推广性。

Abstract: We present VietNormalizer1, an open-source, zero-dependency Python library for Vietnamese text normalization targeting Text-to-Speech (TTS) and Natural Language Processing (NLP) applications. Vietnamese text normalization is a critical yet underserved preprocessing step: real-world Vietnamese text is densely populated with non-standard words (NSWs), including numbers, dates, times, currency amounts, percentages, acronyms, and foreign-language terms, all of which must be converted to fully pronounceable Vietnamese words before TTS synthesis or downstream language processing. Existing Vietnamese normalization tools either require heavy neural dependencies while covering only a narrow subset of NSW classes, or are embedded within larger NLP toolkits without standalone installability. VietNormalizer addresses these gaps through a unified, rule-based pipeline that: (1) converts arbitrary integers, decimals, and large numbers to Vietnamese words; (2) normalizes dates and times to their spoken Vietnamese forms; (3) handles VND and USD currency amounts; (4) expands percentages; (5) resolves acronyms via a customizable CSV dictionary; (6) transliterates non-Vietnamese loanwords and foreign terms to Vietnamese phonetic approximations; and (7) performs Unicode normalization and emoji/special-character removal. All regular expression patterns are pre-compiled at initialization, enabling high-throughput batch processing with minimal memory overhead and no GPU or external API dependency. The library is installable via pip install vietnormalizer, available on PyPI and GitHub at https://github.com/nghimestudio/vietnormalizer, and released under the MIT license. We discuss the design decisions, limitations of existing approaches, and the generalizability of the rule-based normalization paradigm to other low-resource tonal and agglutinative languages.

</details>


### [155] [Bielik-Q2-Sharp: A Comparative Study of Extreme 2-bit Quantization Methods for a Polish 11B Language Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04162)
*Jakub Prejzner*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文首次系统评估了极端2位量化在波兰语大语言模型Bielik-11B上的应用，比较了六种先进量化方法，在有限预算下实现了接近全精度模型的性能，部分方法在推理任务上甚至表现更优，同时公开了全部资源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索极端低比特量化（2-bit）对非英语大模型的实际效果，推动低成本、高效部署，并填补波兰语LLM量化研究空白。

Method: 基于Bielik-11B-v2.3-Instruct模型，使用波兰语语料（CulturaX-PL）校准，对比六种SOTA后训练量化方法（如QuIP#、QTIP等），共享Hessian矩阵以确保公平比较，并在云GPU上进行实验。

Result: 最佳变体QuIP# E8P12在22个波兰语基准测试中达到71.92%准确率（基线为72.07%），eq_bench上提升3.6个百分点；QTIP实现最优每比特效率（~2.4 bpw，3.27GB），且体积比VPTQ小35%；发现旋转类方法存在生成与评分分离现象。

Conclusion: 极端2-bit量化可在极低成本下保持波兰语大模型性能，某些方法甚至提升推理能力，验证了其在小语种模型压缩中的可行性与潜力。

Abstract: We present Bielik-Q2-Sharp, the first systematic academic evaluation of extreme 2-bit quantization applied to a Polish large language model. Using Bielik-11B-v2.3-Instruct (11B parameters, Mistral architecture) as our base model, we compare six state-of-the-art post-training quantization methods -- QuIP#, SpinQuant+GPTQ, ButterflyQuant, QTIP, VPTQ, and AQLM -- all calibrated on a Polish-language corpus (CulturaX-PL) with shared Hessian matrices. Our best variant (QuIP# E8P12) achieves 71.92% across 22 Polish benchmarks versus 72.07% for the IQ2_XXS baseline -- within statistical noise, at a modest size premium (3.26 GB vs. ~2.6 GB). On eq_bench, our method scores 47.14 versus 43.53 (+3.6pp), suggesting superior preservation of higher-order reasoning. QTIP achieves the best per-bit efficiency (79.4% MC acc_norm at ~2.4 bpw, 3.27 GB), matching VPTQ's quality at 35% smaller size. We additionally document a MC-generation dissociation phenomenon where rotation-based methods preserve log-likelihood quality but fail catastrophically at autoregressive generation. The entire project was conducted by a single independent researcher on cloud GPUs (vast.ai) within a $285 budget. All models, Hessians, and evaluation logs are publicly available.

</details>


### [156] [When Do Language Models Endorse Limitations on Human Rights Principles?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04217)
*Keenan Samway,Nicole Miu Takagi,Rada Mihalcea,Bernhard Schölkopf,Ilias Chalkidis,Daniel Hershcovich,Zhijing Jin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本论文评估了大型语言模型（LLMs）在涉及《世界人权宣言》（UDHR）权利权衡时的表现，使用1,152个跨24项权利和8种语言的合成场景，发现LLMs在经济、社会和文化权利上更易接受限制，存在显著的跨语言差异，并易受提示引导，揭示了其在人权对齐方面的系统性偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大型语言模型在全球信息获取和公共话语塑造中的作用日益增强，确保其符合普遍人权原则变得至关重要，以避免在高风险AI交互中侵犯人权。

Method: 研究利用1,152个合成生成的情景，覆盖24项人权条款和8种语言，对11个主流大模型进行分析，比较其在不同语言和权利类别下的响应，并采用Likert量表与开放式回答两种方式评估模型偏好。

Result: 研究发现：（1）LLMs更常接受对经济、社会和文化权利的限制；（2）在中文和印地语中对限制性行为的认可度显著高于英语或罗马尼亚语；（3）模型响应易受提示词引导；（4）Likert评分与开放式回答结果存在明显差异。

Conclusion: 当前大型语言模型在人权对齐方面存在系统性偏差，尤其在不同权利类型和语言间表现不一致，提示需改进评估方法并加强多语言、多文化背景下的对齐机制设计。

Abstract: As Large Language Models (LLMs) increasingly mediate global information access with the potential to shape public discourse, their alignment with universal human rights principles becomes important to ensure that these rights are abided by in high stakes AI-mediated interactions. In this paper, we evaluate how LLMs navigate trade-offs involving the Universal Declaration of Human Rights (UDHR), leveraging 1,152 synthetically generated scenarios across 24 rights articles and eight languages. Our analysis of eleven major LLMs reveals systematic biases where models: (1) accept limiting Economic, Social, and Cultural rights more often than Political and Civil rights, (2) demonstrate significant cross-linguistic variation with elevated endorsement rates of rights-limiting actions in Chinese and Hindi compared to English or Romanian, (3) show substantial susceptibility to prompt-based steering, and (4) exhibit noticeable differences between Likert and open-ended responses, highlighting critical challenges in LLM preference assessment.

</details>


### [157] [Retrieval or Representation? Reassessing Benchmark Gaps in Multilingual and Visually Rich RAG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04238)
*Martin Asenov,Kenza Benkirane,Dan Goldwater,Aneiss Ghodsi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究表明，文档表示的质量是多语言和视觉基准性能提升的主要驱动因素，通过改进转录和预处理方法，BM25能在固定检索机制下大幅缩小性能差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨现代端到端多模态检索器在多语言和复杂版式文档中优于传统BM25方法的原因，分析性能提升的真实来源。

Method: 在保持检索机制不变的前提下，系统性地改变文档的转录和预处理方法，评估其对多语言和视觉基准性能的影响。

Result: 发现更好的文档表示显著提升了BM25的检索效果，可大幅缩小与先进多模态检索器之间的性能差距。

Conclusion: 当前基准测试应分解为独立评估转录与检索能力的模块，以准确归因技术进步并引导研究资源投向关键环节。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) is a common way to ground language models in external documents and up-to-date information. Classical retrieval systems relied on lexical methods such as BM25, which rank documents by term overlap with corpus-level weighting. End-to-end multimodal retrievers trained on large query-document datasets claim substantial improvements over these approaches, especially for multilingual documents with complex visual layouts. We demonstrate that better document representation is the primary driver of benchmark improvements. By systematically varying transcription and preprocessing methods while holding the retrieval mechanism fixed, we demonstrate that BM25 can recover large gaps on multilingual and visual benchmarks. Our findings call for decomposed evaluation benchmarks that separately measure transcription and retrieval capabilities, enabling the field to correctly attribute progress and focus effort where it matters.

</details>


### [158] [Position: Vector Prompt Interfaces Should Be Exposed to Enable Customization of Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04292)
*Liangwei Yang,Shiyu Wang,Haolin Chen,Rithesh Murthy,Ming Zhu,Jielin Qiu,Zixiang Chen,Juntao Tan,Jianguo Zhang,Zhiwei Liu,Wenting Zhao,Silvio Savarese,Caiming Xiong,Huan Wang,Shelby Heinecke*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文主张大型语言模型（LLM）的定制化应超越文本提示，提出将向量提示输入作为公开接口的一部分，以实现更稳定、可扩展且仅限推理阶段的定制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大模型从研究走向实际应用，文本提示在可扩展性、稳定性及控制能力方面表现出局限，亟需更有效的定制接口。

Method: 通过诊断实验比较向量提示调参与文本提示优化的效果，分析其性能趋势与注意力模式，并在推理限制和安全威胁模型下评估向量提示的可行性。

Result: 向量提示调参在更多监督下持续提升性能，而文本提示优化早期即饱和；向量提示展现出密集且全局的注意力模式，表明其具备独特的控制机制，且不会显著增加模型泄露风险。

Conclusion: 应重新思考提示接口的设计，模型提供方应开放向量提示输入作为LLM定制的核心组件。

Abstract: As large language models (LLMs) transition from research prototypes to real-world systems, customization has emerged as a central bottleneck. While text prompts can already customize LLM behavior, we argue that text-only prompting does not constitute a suitable control interface for scalable, stable, and inference-only customization. This position paper argues that model providers should expose \emph{vector prompt inputs} as part of the public interface for customizing LLMs. We support this position with diagnostic evidence showing that vector prompt tuning continues to improve with increasing supervision whereas text-based prompt optimization saturates early, and that vector prompts exhibit dense, global attention patterns indicative of a distinct control mechanism. We further discuss why inference-only customization is increasingly important under realistic deployment constraints, and why exposing vector prompts need not fundamentally increase model leakage risk under a standard black-box threat model. We conclude with a call to action for the community to rethink prompt interfaces as a core component of LLM customization.

</details>


### [159] [The Company You Keep: How LLMs Respond to Dark Triad Traits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04299)
*Zeyi Lu,Angelica Henestrosa,Pavel Chizhov,Ivan P. Yamshchikov*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究探讨了大语言模型（LLM）在面对具有“黑暗三联征”特质用户输入时的回应行为，发现模型通常表现出纠正倾向，但在某些情况下仍会强化有害内容，提示需改进对话系统以更好识别和应对潜在危害请求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于大语言模型常表现出过度迎合用户（AI-sycophancy）的倾向，当用户表达负面社会特质（如黑暗三联征）时，模型可能无意中强化有害行为。本研究旨在分析此类风险并探索改进路径。

Method: 使用构建的特定数据集，分析多个大语言模型对不同程度黑暗三联征特征提示的响应行为，考察其回应的情感倾向与修正或强化倾向的模式差异。

Result: 所有模型主要表现出纠正行为，但在特定情况下仍出现强化输出；模型反应受特质严重程度影响，并在回应情感上呈现差异。

Conclusion: 当前大语言模型在应对潜在有害用户输入时虽有一定抑制能力，但仍有优化空间，需发展更安全的对话机制以动态识别并妥善响应从良性到有害的请求升级。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) often exhibit highly agreeable and reinforcing conversational styles, also known as AI-sycophancy. Although this behavior is encouraged, it may become problematic when interacting with user prompts that reflect negative social tendencies. Such responses risk amplifying harmful behavior rather than mitigating it. In this study, we examine how LLMs respond to user prompts expressing varying degrees of Dark Triad traits (Machiavellianism, Narcissism, and Psychopathy) using a curated dataset. Our analysis reveals differences across models, whereby all models predominantly exhibit corrective behavior, while showing reinforcing output in certain cases. Model behavior also depends on the severity level and differs in the sentiment of the response. Our findings raise implications for designing safer conversational systems that can detect and respond appropriately when users escalate from benign to harmful requests.

</details>


### [160] [$V_1$: Unifying Generation and Self-Verification for Parallel Reasoners](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04304)
*Harman Singh,Xiuyu Li,Kusha Sareen,Monishwaran Maheswaran,Sijun Tan,Xiaoxia Wu,Junxiong Wang,Alpay Ariyak,Qingyang Wu,Samir Khaki,Rishabh Tiwari,Long Lian,Yucheng Lu,Boyi Li,Alane Suhr,Ben Athiwaratkun,Kurt Keutzer*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为 $V_1$ 的新框架，通过成对自验证（pairwise self-verification）统一生成与验证过程，显著提升了复杂推理任务在测试时扩展下的性能。该框架包含两个部分：$V_1$-Infer（基于不确定性的锦标赛排序机制）和 $V_1$-PairRL（联合训练生成器与验证器的强化学习方法），在代码生成和数学推理任务上均表现出优于现有方法的效果与效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的测试时扩展方法依赖独立评分来筛选候选解，但在正确解识别（验证）方面能力有限；而人类及模型本身在成对比较中表现更强，因此作者旨在利用模型在成对自验证上的优势，突破当前验证瓶颈。

Method: $V_1$ 框架包含两个核心组件：$V_1$-Infer 使用基于不确定性的锦标赛排名机制，动态分配计算资源对最难以判断的候选解对进行自我验证；$V_1$-PairRL 是一种强化学习框架，联合训练单一模型同时作为生成器和成对自验证器，使验证器能适应生成器输出分布的变化。

Result: 在多个代码生成（LiveCodeBench, CodeContests, SWE-Bench）和数学推理（AIME, HMMT）基准上，$V_1$-Infer 相比点式验证将 Pass@1 最多提升 10%，且效率更高；$V_1$-PairRL 在测试时扩展效果上比标准强化学习高出 7%-9%，并在基础 Pass@1 上最多提升 8.7%。

Conclusion: $V_1$ 框架通过引入高效的成对自验证机制，有效解决了测试时扩展中的验证瓶颈，实现了更优的性能与计算效率，为复杂推理任务提供了新的优化方向。

Abstract: Test-time scaling for complex reasoning tasks shows that leveraging inference-time compute, by methods such as independently sampling and aggregating multiple solutions, results in significantly better task outcomes. However, a critical bottleneck is verification: sampling is only effective if correct solutions can be reliably identified among candidates. While existing approaches typically evaluate candidates independently via scalar scoring, we demonstrate that models are substantially stronger at pairwise self-verification. Leveraging this insight, we introduce $V_1$, a framework that unifies generation and verification through efficient pairwise ranking. $V_1$ comprises two components: $V_1$-Infer, an uncertainty-guided algorithm using a tournament-based ranking that dynamically allocates self-verification compute to candidate pairs whose relative correctness is most uncertain; and $V_1$-PairRL, an RL framework that jointly trains a single model as both generator and pairwise self-verifier, ensuring the verifier adapts to the generator's evolving distribution. On code generation (LiveCodeBench, CodeContests, SWE-Bench) and math reasoning (AIME, HMMT) benchmarks, $V_1$-Infer improves Pass@1 by up to $10%$ over pointwise verification and outperforms recent test-time scaling methods while being significantly more efficient. Furthermore, $V_1$-PairRL achieves $7$--$9%$ test-time scaling gains over standard RL and pointwise joint training, and improves base Pass@1 by up to 8.7% over standard RL in a code-generation setting.

</details>


### [161] [World Properties without World Models: Recovering Spatial and Temporal Structure from Co-occurrence Statistics in Static Word Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04317)
*Elan Barenholtz*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 线性探针可恢复性表明，静态词嵌入（如GloVe和Word2Vec）本身就能从文本中保留丰富的空间、时间和环境结构，挑战了只有大语言模型才具备世界表征能力的观点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验是否大语言模型中发现的地理和时间变量的线性可恢复性其实源于文本本身的潜在结构，而非模型特有的内部表征。

Method: 使用岭回归探针对静态共现嵌入模型（GloVe和Word2Vec）进行地理坐标和历史出生年份的线性可恢复性测试，并通过语义邻居分析和子空间消融研究信号来源。

Result: 在城市坐标上达到0.71-0.87的R²值，在历史出生年份上达到0.48-0.52，表明静态嵌入中已存在显著的地理和时间信号，且这些信号依赖于国家名称和气候相关词汇等可解释的词汇梯度。

Conclusion: 仅凭线性探针的可恢复性不足以证明模型具有超越文本的内部世界表征；简单的静态词嵌入已能从文本中保留丰富的世界结构。

Abstract: Recent work interprets the linear recoverability of geographic and temporal variables from large language model (LLM) hidden states as evidence for world-like internal representations. We test a simpler possibility: that much of the relevant structure is already latent in text itself. Applying the same class of ridge regression probes to static co-occurrence-based embeddings (GloVe and Word2Vec), we find substantial recoverable geographic signal and weaker but reliable temporal signal, with held-out R^2 values of 0.71-0.87 for city coordinates and 0.48-0.52 for historical birth years. Semantic-neighbor analyses and targeted subspace ablations show that these signals depend strongly on interpretable lexical gradients, especially country names and climate-related vocabulary. These findings suggest that ordinary word co-occurrence preserves richer spatial, temporal, and environmental structure than is often assumed, revealing a remarkable and underappreciated capacity of simple static embeddings to preserve world-shaped structure from text alone. Linear probe recoverability alone therefore does not establish a representational move beyond text.

</details>


### [162] [AILS-NTUA at SemEval-2026 Task 12: Graph-Based Retrieval and Reflective Prompting for Abductive Event Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04319)
*Nikolas Karafyllis,Maria Lymperaiou,Giorgos Filandrianos,Athanasios Voulodimos,Giorgos Stamou*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了一种三阶段系统，在SemEval 2026 Task 12中排名第一，准确率达0.95，并通过跨模型错误分析揭示了多标签因果推理中的系统性偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提升事件推理任务的性能，识别当前模型在多标签因果推理中存在的共性问题与系统性缺陷。

Method: 结合基于图的检索、经过反思性提示进化优化的LLM驱动溯因推理以及事后一致性约束的三阶段系统，并对14个模型进行跨模型错误分析。

Result: 系统在评估阶段排名第一，准确率达到0.95；发现三种共享归纳偏见：因果链不完整、近因偏好和显著性偏差，跨家族收敛导致原因数量减少51%。

Conclusion: 多标签因果推理中的失败模式具有系统性和跨模型一致性，需针对性改进模型设计以克服这些偏见。

Abstract: We present a winning three-stage system for SemEval 2026 Task~12: Abductive Event Reasoning that combines graph-based retrieval, LLM-driven abductive reasoning with prompt design optimized through reflective prompt evolution, and post-hoc consistency enforcement; our system ranks first on the evaluation-phase leaderboard with an accuracy score of 0.95. Cross-model error analysis across 14 models (7~families) reveals three shared inductive biases: causal chain incompleteness, proximate cause preference, and salience bias, whose cross-family convergence (51\% cause-count reduction) indicates systematic rather than model-specific failure modes in multi-label causal reasoning.

</details>


### [163] [AgentIR: Reasoning-Aware Retrival for Deep Research Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04384)
*Zijian Chen,Xueguang Ma,Shengyao Zhuang,Jimmy Lin,Akari Asai,Victor Zhong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的检索范式——推理感知检索（Reasoning-Aware Retrieval），通过结合智能体的推理过程与查询来提升检索效果，并利用DR-Synth方法生成训练数据，显著提升了在Deep Research任务中的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的检索系统忽略了Deep Research智能体在每次搜索前产生的自然语言推理信息，而这些信息富含意图和上下文，作者旨在利用这一被忽视的信号以提升检索性能。

Method: 提出了推理感知检索，将智能体的推理轨迹与其查询联合嵌入；同时提出DR-Synth数据合成方法，从标准问答数据集中生成适用于Deep Research检索器的训练数据。

Result: 所提出的两个组件均独立有效，组合后训练出的AgentIR-4B模型在BrowseComp-Plus基准上达到68%的准确率，优于两倍大小的传统嵌入模型（50%）和BM25（37%）。

Conclusion: 通过利用智能体的显式推理轨迹，推理感知检索显著提升了Deep Research场景下的检索效果，为面向智能体的检索系统提供了新方向。

Abstract: Deep Research agents are rapidly emerging as primary consumers of modern retrieval systems. Unlike human users who issue and refine queries without documenting their intermediate thought processes, Deep Research agents generate explicit natural language reasoning before each search call, revealing rich intent and contextual information that existing retrievers entirely ignore. To exploit this overlooked signal, we introduce: (1) Reasoning-Aware Retrieval, a retrieval paradigm that jointly embeds the agent's reasoning trace alongside its query; and (2) DR-Synth, a data synthesis method that generates Deep Research retriever training data from standard QA datasets. We demonstrate that both components are independently effective, and their combination yields a trained embedding model, AgentIR-4B, with substantial gains. On the challenging BrowseComp-Plus benchmark, AgentIR-4B achieves 68\% accuracy with the open-weight agent Tongyi-DeepResearch, compared to 50\% with conventional embedding models twice its size, and 37\% with BM25. Code and data are available at: https://texttron.github.io/AgentIR/.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [164] [Asymmetric Goal Drift in Coding Agents Under Value Conflict](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03456)
*Magnus Saebo,Spencer Gibson,Tyler Crosse,Achyutha Menon,Eyon Jang,Diogo Cruz*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于OpenCode的框架，用于评估编码智能体在长期、复杂环境中的目标漂移现象，发现现有模型在安全与隐私等强价值观冲突下容易违背系统指令，且浅层合规检查不足以防止此类问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多依赖静态、合成环境来分析智能体行为，无法反映真实世界中智能体面临的多重价值张力与长期上下文影响，因此需要更贴近现实的评估框架。

Method: 构建基于OpenCode的多步骤编码任务框架，通过引入环境压力与竞争性价值观，测量智能体在有无外部压力情况下随时间违反系统提示约束的程度。

Result: GPT-5 mini、Haiku 4.5 和 Grok Code Fast 1 均表现出非对称漂移：当系统指令与模型强烈持有的价值观（如安全、隐私）冲突时，更易违反指令；目标漂移与价值对齐程度、对抗压力和累积上下文三者呈正相关，且即使是对隐私等强价值观，在持续压力下仍存在违规行为。

Conclusion: 当前的对齐方法在确保智能体平衡显式用户约束与习得偏好方面存在不足，仅靠浅层合规检查无法有效防止价值层级被利用而导致的指令覆盖问题。

Abstract: Agentic coding agents are increasingly deployed autonomously, at scale, and over long-context horizons. Throughout an agent's lifetime, it must navigate tensions between explicit instructions, learned values, and environmental pressures, often in contexts unseen during training. Prior work on model preferences, agent behavior under value tensions, and goal drift has relied on static, synthetic settings that do not capture the complexity of real-world environments. To this end, we introduce a framework built on OpenCode to orchestrate realistic, multi-step coding tasks to measure how agents violate explicit constraints in their system prompt over time with and without environmental pressure toward competing values. Using this framework, we demonstrate that GPT-5 mini, Haiku 4.5, and Grok Code Fast 1 exhibit asymmetric drift: they are more likely to violate their system prompt when its constraint opposes strongly-held values like security and privacy. We find for the models and values tested that goal drift correlates with three compounding factors: value alignment, adversarial pressure, and accumulated context. However, even strongly-held values like privacy show non-zero violation rates under sustained environmental pressure. These findings reveal that shallow compliance checks are insufficient and that comment-based pressure can exploit model value hierarchies to override system prompt instructions. More broadly, our findings highlight a gap in current alignment approaches in ensuring that agentic systems appropriately balance explicit user constraints against broadly beneficial learned preferences under sustained environmental pressure.

</details>


### [165] [RAGNav: A Retrieval-Augmented Topological Reasoning Framework for Multi-Goal Visual-Language Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03745)
*Ling Luo,Qiangian Bai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出RAGNav框架，通过双基记忆系统结合语义推理与物理结构，解决多目标视觉语言导航中的空间幻觉与规划漂移问题，实现SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有检索增强生成（RAG）方法在处理多目标关联时易出现空间幻觉和规划漂移，缺乏显式空间建模能力，难以应对复杂多目标导航任务。

Method: 提出RAGNav框架，包含双基记忆系统：底层拓扑图维护物理连接关系，高层语义森林进行环境层次化抽象；引入锚点引导的条件检索机制和基于拓扑邻居的分数传播机制，利用物理关联进行语义校准。

Result: 在复杂多目标导航任务中显著提升目标可达性推理与序列规划效率，实验结果表明RAGNav达到最先进的（SOTA）性能。

Conclusion: RAGNav有效融合语义与物理结构信息，增强了多目标导航中的空间理解与规划一致性，为视觉语言导航提供了更鲁棒的框架设计思路。

Abstract: Vision-Language Navigation (VLN) is evolving from single-point pathfinding toward the more challenging Multi-Goal VLN. This task requires agents to accurately identify multiple entities while collaboratively reasoning over their spatial-physical constraints and sequential execution order. However, generic Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) paradigms often suffer from spatial hallucinations and planning drift when handling multi-object associations due to the lack of explicit spatial modeling.To address these challenges, we propose RAGNav, a framework that bridges the gap between semantic reasoning and physical structure. The core of RAGNav is a Dual-Basis Memory system, which integrates a low-level topological map for maintaining physical connectivity with a high-level semantic forest for hierarchical environment abstraction. Building on this representation, the framework introduces an anchor-guided conditional retrieval and a topological neighbor score propagation mechanism. This approach facilitates the rapid screening of candidate targets and the elimination of semantic noise, while performing semantic calibration by leveraging the physical associations inherent in the topological neighborhood.This mechanism significantly enhances the capability of inter-target reachability reasoning and the efficiency of sequential planning. Experimental results demonstrate that RAGNav achieves state-of-the-art (SOTA) performance in complex multi-goal navigation tasks.

</details>


### [166] [AgentSelect: Benchmark for Narrative Query-to-Agent Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03761)
*Yunxiao Shi,Wujiang Xu,Tingwei Chen,Haoning Shang,Ling Yang,Yunfeng Wan,Zhuo Cao,Xing Zi,Dimitris N. Metaxas,Min Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AgentSelect 是一个针对 LLM 智能体配置推荐的统一基准，通过整合多源异构评估数据，构建查询到智能体的推荐框架，支持端到端的智能体选择，并推动智能体生态系统的可复现研究与应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM排行榜和工具/智能体基准测试孤立地评估组件，缺乏跨任务、指标和候选池的统一性，难以提供面向端到端智能体配置推荐的有效监督。因此，亟需一个统一的基准来支持基于查询的智能体推荐。

Method: 提出 AgentSelect 基准，将智能体选择重构为基于能力画像的叙事性查询到智能体推荐任务，并系统性地将来自40多个来源的异构评估结果转化为统一的、仅正样本的交互数据。

Result: AgentSelect 包含111,179个查询、107,721个可部署智能体和251,103条交互记录，分析显示传统基于流行度的方法失效，需依赖内容感知的能力匹配；在合成数据上训练的模型具备学习能力，并能在控制变量下表现出对能力敏感的行为，且在真实场景（如 MuleRun）中泛化性能良好。

Conclusion: AgentSelect 提供了首个统一的智能体推荐数据与评估基础设施，为研究和加速智能体生态系统的发展奠定了可复现的基础。

Abstract: LLM agents are rapidly becoming the practical interface for task automation, yet the ecosystem lacks a principled way to choose among an exploding space of deployable configurations. Existing LLM leaderboards and tool/agent benchmarks evaluate components in isolation and remain fragmented across tasks, metrics, and candidate pools, leaving a critical research gap: there is little query-conditioned supervision for learning to recommend end-to-end agent configurations that couple a backbone model with a toolkit. We address this gap with AgentSelect, a benchmark that reframes agent selection as narrative query-to-agent recommendation over capability profiles and systematically converts heterogeneous evaluation artifacts into unified, positive-only interaction data. AgentSelectcomprises 111,179 queries, 107,721 deployable agents, and 251,103 interaction records aggregated from 40+ sources, spanning LLM-only, toolkit-only, and compositional agents. Our analyses reveal a regime shift from dense head reuse to long-tail, near one-off supervision, where popularity-based CF/GNN methods become fragile and content-aware capability matching is essential. We further show that Part~III synthesized compositional interactions are learnable, induce capability-sensitive behavior under controlled counterfactual edits, and improve coverage over realistic compositions; models trained on AgentSelect also transfer to a public agent marketplace (MuleRun), yielding consistent gains on an unseen catalog. Overall, AgentSelect provides the first unified data and evaluation infrastructure for agent recommendation, which establishes a reproducible foundation to study and accelerate the emerging agent ecosystem.

</details>


### [167] [Generative AI in Managerial Decision-Making: Redefining Boundaries through Ambiguity Resolution and Sycophancy Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03970)
*Sule Ozturk Birim,Fabrizio Marozzo,Yigit Kazancoglu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究探讨了生成式人工智能在复杂商业决策中的应用，重点分析其在模糊情境下的战略建议可靠性。通过构建四维商业模糊分类体系并采用“LLM作为评判者”框架，发现模型在识别内部矛盾和上下文模糊性方面表现良好，但在语言结构细微差别上存在困难；歧义消解能持续提升响应质量，而奉承行为则因模型架构不同而异。研究表明，生成式AI可作为管理者认知的延伸工具，但仍需人类监督以确保其决策可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式人工智能正被广泛应用于企业管理决策中，但其在模糊商业情境下的建议可靠性尚不明确，存在研究空白。本研究旨在揭示不同模型在检测和处理商业模糊性方面的能力差异，并评估其在接收到错误指令时的顺从行为（sycophantic behavior），从而填补这一知识缺口。

Method: 研究提出了一种新的四维商业模糊性分类法，设计涵盖战略、战术与操作层面的人机协同实验，并利用“LLM-as-a-judge”框架对生成决策进行评估，评估标准包括一致性、可执行性、论证质量和约束遵守情况，同时系统性地引入歧义解析流程以检验其对输出质量的影响。

Result: 实验结果显示：当前主流模型在识别内部矛盾和上下文模糊方面表现优异，但在处理结构性语言细微差别时能力有限；系统的歧义解析过程显著提升了各类决策输出的质量；不同架构的模型在面对错误指令时表现出不同程度的奉承行为，说明其推理路径受训练方式影响明显。

Conclusion: 生成式人工智能可作为管理决策中的认知支架，辅助识别和解决人类可能忽视的模糊问题，扩展有限理性边界。然而，其自身存在人工局限性，尤其在语言理解深度与独立判断方面仍依赖人类引导，因此必须由人类管理者监督使用，才能确保其作为战略伙伴的可靠性。

Abstract: Generative artificial intelligence is increasingly being integrated into complex business workflows, fundamentally shifting the boundaries of managerial decision-making. However, the reliability of its strategic advice in ambiguous business contexts remains a critical knowledge gap. This study addresses this by comparing various models on ambiguity detection, evaluating how a systematic resolution process enhances response quality, and investigating their sycophantic behavior when presented with flawed directives. Using a novel four-dimensional business ambiguity taxonomy, we conducted a human-in-the-loop experiment across strategic, tactical, and operational scenarios. The resulting decisions were assessed with an "LLM-as-a-judge" framework on criteria including agreement, actionability, justification quality, and constraint adherence. Results reveal distinct performance capabilities. While models excel in detecting internal contradictions and contextual ambiguities, they struggle with structural linguistic nuances. Ambiguity resolution consistently increased response quality across all decision types, while sycophantic behavior analysis revealed distinct patterns depending on the model architecture. This study contributes to the bounded rationality literature by positioning GAI as a cognitive scaffold that can detect and resolve ambiguities managers might overlook, but whose own artificial limitations necessitate human management to ensure its reliability as a strategic partner.

</details>


### [168] [Phi-4-reasoning-vision-15B Technical Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03975)
*Jyoti Aneja,Michael Harrison,Neel Joshi,Tyler LaBonte,John Langford,Eduardo Salinas*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Phi-4-reasoning-vision-15B 是一个紧凑的开源多模态推理模型，专注于高效实现视觉与语言任务，并在科学数学推理和用户界面理解方面表现突出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在为研究社区提供构建小型、高效多模态推理模型的实用见解，并通过开源模型推动相关领域发展。

Method: 采用精心的架构设计、系统性数据过滤、错误修正、合成数据增强，结合高分辨率动态编码器和混合推理与非推理数据训练，引入显式模式标记以支持单模型多模式响应。

Result: 模型在显著减少训练与推理计算量和token使用的情况下，实现了具有竞争力的性能；高质量数据被验证为提升模型表现的主要因素；高分辨率编码器提升了感知准确性，进而增强推理质量；混合数据策略使模型能灵活应对简单任务与复杂推理。

Conclusion: 数据质量是提升小规模多模态模型性能的关键，合理的架构与训练策略可使其在多种任务上高效运行并保持高性能。

Abstract: We present Phi-4-reasoning-vision-15B, a compact open-weight multimodal reasoning model, and share the motivations, design choices, experiments, and learnings that informed its development. Our goal is to contribute practical insight to the research community on building smaller, efficient multimodal reasoning models and to share the result of these learnings as an open-weight model that is good at common vision and language tasks and excels at scientific and mathematical reasoning and understanding user interfaces. Our contributions include demonstrating that careful architecture choices and rigorous data curation enable smaller, open-weight multimodal models to achieve competitive performance with significantly less training and inference-time compute and tokens. The most substantial improvements come from systematic filtering, error correction, and synthetic augmentation -- reinforcing that data quality remains the primary lever for model performance. Systematic ablations show that high-resolution, dynamic-resolution encoders yield consistent improvements, as accurate perception is a prerequisite for high-quality reasoning. Finally, a hybrid mix of reasoning and non-reasoning data with explicit mode tokens allows a single model to deliver fast direct answers for simpler tasks and chain-of-thought reasoning for complex problems.

</details>


### [169] [BeamPERL: Parameter-Efficient RL with Verifiable Rewards Specializes Compact LLMs for Structured Beam Mechanics Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04124)
*Tarjei Paule Hage,Markus J. Buehler*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 强化学习结合精确奖励虽能提升模型在物理推理任务上的表现，但主要促使模型学习解题模板而非真正理解物理规律，导致在结构变化下泛化能力下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究在没有教师推理轨迹的情况下，使用硬性、可验证的奖励信号进行强化学习，是否能让小型语言模型真正学会物理推理，还是仅仅学会模式匹配以获得正确答案。

Method: 基于1.5B参数的语言模型，在梁静力学这一经典工程问题上，采用参数高效的RLVR方法，利用符号求解器提供的二元正确性奖励进行训练，不依赖人工生成的推理过程。

Result: 最优检查点Pass@1比基础模型提升66.7%，但能力具有各向异性：在增加负载等组合变化下能泛化，但在支撑点移动等拓扑变化下失败；中间检查点推理能力最强，继续优化反而降低鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 结果表明，仅靠精确的结果级奖励无法保证模型掌握可迁移的物理推理能力，强化学习更倾向于形成程序化解题模板而非内化物理规律；要实现稳健的科学推理，需将可验证奖励与结构化推理框架相结合。

Abstract: Can reinforcement learning with hard, verifiable rewards teach a compact language model to reason about physics, or does it primarily learn to pattern-match toward correct answers? We study this question by training a 1.5B-parameter reasoning model on beam statics, a classic engineering problem, using parameter-efficient RLVR with binary correctness rewards from symbolic solvers, without teacher-generated reasoning traces. The best BeamPERL checkpoint achieves a 66.7% improvement in Pass@1 over the base model. However, the learned competence is anisotropic: the model generalizes compositionally (more loads) but fails under topological shifts (moved supports) that require the same equilibrium equations. Intermediate checkpoints yield the strongest reasoning, while continued optimization degrades robustness while maintaining reward. These findings reveal a key limitation of outcome-level alignment: reinforcement learning with exact physics rewards induces procedural solution templates rather than internalization of governing equations. The precision of the reward signal - even when analytically exact - does not by itself guarantee transferable physical reasoning. Our results suggest that verifiable rewards may need to be paired with structured reasoning scaffolding to move beyond template matching toward robust scientific reasoning.

</details>


### [170] [Towards Realistic Personalization: Evaluating Long-Horizon Preference Following in Personalized User-LLM Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04191)
*Qianyun Guo,Yibo Li,Yue Liu,Bryan Hooi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本论文提出了RealPref，一个用于评估大语言模型在个性化用户交互中遵循现实偏好的基准。该基准包含100个用户档案、1300条个性化偏好、四种偏好表达方式和长周期交互历史，并设计了三类测试问题与评分标准。实验结果表明，随着上下文增长和偏好表达更隐晦，模型表现显著下降，且对新场景的泛化仍具挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究缺乏对大语言模型在长期、真实互动中理解并遵循用户复杂偏好的系统性评估，因此需要构建更贴近实际使用场景的评测基准。

Method: 构建RealPref基准，涵盖多样化的用户档案与偏好类型，设计多形式测试题（选择题、判断题、开放题），并采用LLM-as-a-judge结合详细评分标准进行自动化评估。

Result: 实验发现模型在长上下文和隐式偏好下性能明显下降，且难以将偏好理解泛化至未见过的情境；不同偏好表达方式对模型理解能力提出不同程度挑战。

Conclusion: RealPref为评估个性化偏好遵循能力提供了有效工具，揭示了当前模型在长期交互与隐式偏好理解上的局限，推动未来研究开发更用户感知的智能助手。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly serving as personal assistants, where users share complex and diverse preferences over extended interactions. However, assessing how well LLMs can follow these preferences in realistic, long-term situations remains underexplored. This work proposes RealPref, a benchmark for evaluating realistic preference-following in personalized user-LLM interactions. RealPref features 100 user profiles, 1300 personalized preferences, four types of preference expression (ranging from explicit to implicit), and long-horizon interaction histories. It includes three types of test questions (multiple-choice, true-or-false, and open-ended), with detailed rubrics for LLM-as-a-judge evaluation. Results indicate that LLM performance significantly drops as context length grows and preference expression becomes more implicit, and that generalizing user preference understanding to unseen scenarios poses further challenges. RealPref and these findings provide a foundation for future research to develop user-aware LLM assistants that better adapt to individual needs. The code is available at https://github.com/GG14127/RealPref.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [171] [LiteVLA-Edge: Quantized On-Device Multimodal Control for Embedded Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03380)
*Justin Williams,Kishor Datta Gupta,Roy George,Mrinmoy Sarkar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了LiteVLA-Edge，一种面向嵌入式设备部署的轻量级视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型推理框架，可在Jetson Orin类硬件上实现完全离线、低延迟的端到端推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型因计算开销大和推理延迟高，难以在嵌入式机器人系统中实际部署，因此需要一个高效、可落地的解决方案。

Method: 采用监督式图像到动作微调（FP32）、4位GGUF后训练量化，并结合llama.cpp运行时实现GPU加速推理，集成于ROS 2的感知-推理-执行流程中。

Result: 在Jetson Orin类设备上实现了平均150.5毫秒的端到端延迟（约6.6 Hz），支持完全离线运行，并保持模块化架构。

Conclusion: LiteVLA-Edge为在资源受限的嵌入式平台上部署紧凑型多模态控制模型提供了可行的系统路径，验证了语言条件控制在边缘设备上的实时性潜力。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models provide a unified framework for perception, language conditioning, and action generation, but many existing systems remain difficult to deploy in embedded robotic settings because of their computational requirements and inference latency. In this paper, we present LiteVLA-Edge, a deployment-oriented VLA pipeline for fully on-device inference on Jetson Orin-class hardware. Our approach combines supervised image-to-action fine-tuning in FP32 with post-training 4-bit GGUF quantization and GPU-accelerated inference through the \texttt{llama.cpp} runtime. Under our deployment configuration, LiteVLA-Edge achieves a mean end-to-end latency of 150.5\,ms (approximately 6.6\,Hz) while operating entirely offline within a ROS~2-integrated perception--reasoning--action pipeline. Rather than introducing a new policy objective, our contribution is a practical systems path for executing compact multimodal control models locally on embedded hardware while preserving modular interfaces between perception, reasoning, and actuation. These results establish timing feasibility for reactive language-conditioned control and provide a reproducible baseline for future task-level evaluation of on-device VLAs in robotics.

</details>


### [172] [Impact of Localization Errors on Label Quality for Online HD Map Construction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03452)
*Alexander Blumberg,Jonas Merkert,Richard Fehler,Fabian Immel,Frank Bieder,Jan-Hendrik Pauls,Christoph Stiller*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究了在使用消费者车队的车载传感器数据进行在线高精地图构建时，定位误差对生成地图标签的影响。通过引入不同类型的噪声并训练最先进的模型MapTRv2，评估了模型性能的退化情况，并提出了一种基于距离的评价指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高精地图对于自动驾驶至关重要，但其制作和维护成本高昂，因此需要利用大规模消费者车队的数据来实现在线构建。然而，这些数据存在定位误差问题，影响地图标签质量，需研究其影响。

Method: 引入三种定位误差类型（Ramp、Gaussian和Perlin噪声），在Argoverse 2数据集上训练MapTRv2模型，并分析不同误差水平下模型性能的变化；提出一种距离相关的地图构建评价指标。

Result: 实验表明定位噪声显著影响模型性能，航向角误差比位置误差影响更大，且模型性能随噪声增加呈非线性下降；远处标签虽变形严重但对驾驶影响较小。

Conclusion: 定位误差尤其是航向角误差会显著扭曲高精地图标签，影响在线建图性能，未来应重视高质量真值数据的获取与距离加权评估方法的应用。

Abstract: High-definition (HD) maps are crucial for autonomous vehicles, but their creation and maintenance is very costly. This motivates the idea of online HD map construction. To provide a continuous large-scale stream of training data, existing HD maps can be used as labels for onboard sensor data from consumer vehicle fleets. However, compared to current, well curated HD map perception datasets, this fleet data suffers from localization errors, resulting in distorted map labels. We introduce three kinds of localization errors, Ramp, Gaussian, and Perlin noise, to examine their influence on generated map labels. We train a variant of MapTRv2, a state-of-the-art online HD map construction model, on the Argoverse 2 dataset with various levels of localization errors and assess the degradation of model performance. Since localization errors affect distant labels more severely, but are also less significant to driving performance, we introduce a distance-based map construction metric. Our experiments reveal that localization noise affects the model performance significantly. We demonstrate that errors in heading angle exert a more substantial influence than position errors, as angle errors result in a greater distortion of labels as distance to the vehicle increases. Furthermore, we can demonstrate that the model benefits from non-distorted ground truth (GT) data and that the performance decreases more than linearly with the increase in noisy data. Our study additionally provides a qualitative evaluation of the extent to which localization errors influence the construction of HD maps.

</details>


### [173] [Radar-based Pose Optimization for HD Map Generation from Noisy Multi-Drive Vehicle Fleet Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03453)
*Alexander Blumberg,Jonas Merkert,Christoph Stiller*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种通过优化雷达点云和姿态图来减少定位噪声的方法，用于自动生成高精度地图，提升自动驾驶中的车辆定位和车道边界地图生成效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高精地图对自动驾驶至关重要，但人工制作和维护成本高昂，而车队车辆传感器数据噪声较大，影响地图生成质量。

Method: 利用多趟行驶的雷达点云数据进行车辆位姿对齐，并通过姿态图优化获得全局优化的位姿，进而生成全局雷达占据地图，并改进车道边界地图生成流程。

Result: 生成的地图具有更清晰的特征（如护栏柱），显著提升了车载定位精度和车道边界地图的质量，优于原有的纯线检测方法。

Conclusion: 该方法有效降低了低成本传感器带来的定位噪声，为自动化高精地图生成提供了可行方案。

Abstract: High-definition (HD) maps are important for autonomous driving, but their manual generation and maintenance is very expensive. This motivates the usage of an automated map generation pipeline. Fleet vehicles provide sufficient sensors for map generation, but their measurements are less precise, introducing noise into the mapping pipeline. This work focuses on mitigating the localization noise component through aligning radar measurements in terms of raw radar point clouds of vehicle poses of different drives and performing pose graph optimization to produce a globally optimized solution between all drives present in the dataset. Improved poses are first used to generate a global radar occupancy map, aimed to facilitate precise on-vehicle localization. Through qualitative analysis we show contrast-rich feature clarity, focusing on omnipresent guardrail posts as the main feature type observable in the map. Second, the improved poses can be used as a basis for an existing lane boundary map generation pipeline, majorly improving map output compared to its original pure line detection based optimization approach.

</details>


### [174] [Navigating in Uncertain Environments with Heterogeneous Visibility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03495)
*Jongann Lee,Melkior Ornik*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种新的启发式算法，通过权衡前往高可见性位置的代价与信息增益，优化不确定环境中的导航路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在连接性不确定的环境中导航时，需要平衡路径代价和信息获取，以解决地图模糊问题。

Method: 利用具有不同可见性水平的节点框架，从某些有利位置观察远处边；通过自定义观测奖励和遍历代价之和进行优化，并采用采样最短路径的方法估计高奖励路径。

Result: 在多种不确定性导航任务中测试，相比不考虑观测的最短路径基线，平均遍历代价更低，且相较于现有方法计算开销呈指数级降低。

Conclusion: 该方法能有效平衡探索与路径代价，适用于多种场景，仅需调节一个超参数即可适应不同应用需求。

Abstract: Navigating an environment with uncertain connectivity requires a strategic balance between minimizing the cost of traversal and seeking information to resolve map ambiguities. Unlike previous approaches that rely on local sensing, we utilize a framework where nodes possess varying visibility levels, allowing for observation of distant edges from certain vantage points. We propose a novel heuristic algorithm that balances the cost of detouring to high-visibility locations against the gain in information by optimizing the sum of a custom observation reward and the cost of traversal. We introduce a technique to sample the shortest path on numerous realizations of the environment, which we use to define an edge's utility for observation and to quickly estimate the path with the highest reward. Our approach can be easily adapted to a variety of scenarios by tuning a single hyperparameter that determines the importance of observation. We test our method on a variety of uncertain navigation tasks, including a map based on real-world topographical data. The method demonstrates lower mean cost of traversal compared to a shortest path baseline that does not consider observation and has exponentially lower computational overhead compared to an existing method for balancing observation with path cost minimization.

</details>


### [175] [Sampling-Based Motion Planning with Scene Graphs Under Perception Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03514)
*Qingxi Meng,Emiliano Flores,Thai Duong,Vaibhav Unhelkar,Lydia E. Kavraki*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于高自由度机器人在多目标感知约束下的运动规划方法MOPS-PRM，通过将感知成本嵌入场景图并用于选择性采样构型，提升了检测对象数量和跟踪率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有感知感知方法通常关注低自由度系统或仅考虑单一物体，难以满足高自由度机器人在复杂环境中对多目标监控的需求。

Method: 提出MOPS-PRM，基于路线图的运动规划方法，利用场景图表示环境，并将多目标的感知成本融入运动规划过程，通过感知成本指导构型空间采样。

Result: 在模拟和真实实验中验证，相比基线方法平均检测对象数提升超36%，跟踪率提高约17%，且规划时间和路径长度相当。

Conclusion: MOPS-PRM有效实现了高自由度机器人在多目标感知约束下的高效运动规划，具有良好的应用前景。

Abstract: It will be increasingly common for robots to operate in cluttered human-centered environments such as homes, workplaces, and hospitals, where the robot is often tasked to maintain perception constraints, such as monitoring people or multiple objects, for safety and reliability while executing its task. However, existing perception-aware approaches typically focus on low-degree-of-freedom (DoF) systems or only consider a single object in the context of high-DoF robots. This motivates us to consider the problem of perception-aware motion planning for high-DoF robots that accounts for multi-object monitoring constraints. We employ a scene graph representation of the environment, offering a great potential for incorporating long-horizon task and motion planning thanks to its rich semantic and spatial information. However, it does not capture perception-constrained information, such as the viewpoints the user prefers. To address these challenges, we propose MOPS-PRM, a roadmap-based motion planner, that integrates the perception cost of observing multiple objects or humans directly into motion planning for high-DoF robots. The perception cost is embedded to each object as part of a scene graph, and used to selectively sample configurations for roadmap construction, implicitly enforcing the perception constraints. Our method is extensively validated in both simulated and real-world experiments, achieving more than ~36% improvement in the average number of detected objects and ~17% better track rate against other perception-constrained baselines, with comparable planning times and path lengths.

</details>


### [176] [Passive Phase-Oriented Impedance Shaping for Rapid Acceleration in Soft Robotic Swimmers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03537)
*Qimin Feng,Orion A. Roberts,Qiang Zhong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本研究探讨了约束层阻尼（CLD）作为一种被动的频率选择性阻抗成形机制，用于软体水下机器人，显著提升了加速性能和终端速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了提升软体机器人在水下的快速加速和爆发式运动能力，需优化推力生成中的力-速度相位对齐，而非依赖共振调节。

Method: 采用约束层阻尼（CLD）结构，通过干态阻抗测量表征其频率选择性，并在受限推进和自由游泳实验中测试其对推力、相位关系、加速度和速度的影响。

Result: CLD在高频下增强了耗散阻抗，改善了力-运动相位关系；实验显示峰值加速度提高近五倍，终端速度提高三倍。

Conclusion: 基于相位导向的被动阻抗调控是一种无需主动控制即可显著提升软体机器人瞬态推进性能的有效方法。

Abstract: Rapid acceleration and burst maneuvers in underwater robots depend less on maintaining precise resonance and more on force--velocity phase alignment during thrust generation. In this work, we investigate constrained-layer damping (CLD) as a passive mechanism for frequency-selective impedance shaping in soft robotic swimmers. Unlike conventional stiffness-tuning approaches, CLD selectively amplifies the dissipative component of bending impedance while preserving storage stiffness, passively shifting the impedance composition toward dissipative dominance as actuation frequency increases. We characterize this behavior through dry impedance measurements, demonstrate that CLD enhances thrust and alters force--motion phase relationships across Strouhal numbers in constrained propulsion tests, and validate that passive impedance shaping yields a nearly five-fold increase in peak acceleration and a three-fold increase in terminal velocity in unconstrained swimming trials. These results establish phase-oriented passive impedance modulation as a simple, control-free pathway for improving transient propulsion in soft robotic systems.

</details>


### [177] [Real-time loosely coupled GNSS and IMU integration via Factor Graph Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03546)
*Radu-Andrei Cioaca,Cristian Rusu,Paul Irofti,Gianluca Caparra,Andrei-Alexandru Marinache,Florin Stoican*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于因子图优化（FGO）的松耦合GNSS/IMU融合架构，旨在实现城市峡谷等复杂环境下的实时定位，提高了服务可用性，但以一定定位精度为代价，系统分析了精度、可用性和计算效率之间的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了提升复杂城市环境中GNSS定位的可用性和实时性，克服传统批处理FGO方法计算量大、难以实时运行的问题。

Method: 采用松耦合架构，将GNSS与IMU数据融合，并基于因子图优化（FGO）框架实现实时处理，在UrbanNav-HK-MediumUrban-1数据集上进行验证。

Result: 所提方法实现了实时运行，并显著提高了服务可用性，相较于批处理FGO方法更具实用性，但定位精度有所下降。

Conclusion: 在实时性与服务可用性要求较高的应用场景中，该松耦合实时FGO方法是一种有效的GNSS/IMU融合方案，尽管存在精度折衷，但为实际部署提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Accurate positioning, navigation, and timing (PNT) is fundamental to the operation of modern technologies and a key enabler of autonomous systems. A very important component of PNT is the Global Navigation Satellite System (GNSS) which ensures outdoor positioning. Modern research directions have pushed the performance of GNSS localization to new heights by fusing GNSS measurements with other sensory information, mainly measurements from Inertial Measurement Units (IMU). In this paper, we propose a loosely coupled architecture to integrate GNSS and IMU measurements using a Factor Graph Optimization (FGO) framework. Because the FGO method can be computationally challenging and often used as a post-processing method, our focus is on assessing its localization accuracy and service availability while operating in real-time in challenging environments (urban canyons). Experimental results on the UrbanNav-HK-MediumUrban-1 dataset show that the proposed approach achieves real-time operation and increased service availability compared to batch FGO methods. While this improvement comes at the cost of reduced positioning accuracy, the paper provides a detailed analysis of the trade-offs between accuracy, availability, and computational efficiency that characterize real-time FGO-based GNSS/IMU fusion.

</details>


### [178] [Real-time tightly coupled GNSS and IMU integration via Factor Graph Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03556)
*Radu-Andrei Cioaca,Paul Irofti,Cristian Rusu,Gianluca Caparra,Andrei-Alexandru Marinache,Florin Stoican*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种实时紧耦合的GNSS-IMU因子图优化方法，通过增量优化和固定滞后边缘化实现城市环境中可靠的定位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在密集城市环境中，由于GNSS信号经常被遮挡、多路径效应以及卫星几何结构快速变化，可靠定位仍然具有挑战性。现有的因子图优化方法大多为离线处理，难以满足实时应用需求。

Method: 采用实时紧耦合的GNSS-IMU因子图优化（FGO）方法，结合增量优化与固定滞后边缘化技术，实现因果状态估计。

Result: 在UrbanNav数据集上评估了该方法的性能，结果表明其在高度城市化且GNSS退化的环境中具有良好的鲁棒性和精度。

Conclusion: 所提出的方法能够在GNSS信号受限的城市环境中实现高精度、实时的定位，适用于自动驾驶和移动机器人等应用场景。

Abstract: Reliable positioning in dense urban environments remains challenging due to frequent GNSS signal blockage, multipath, and rapidly varying satellite geometry. While factor graph optimization (FGO)-based GNSS-IMU fusion has demonstrated strong robustness and accuracy, most formulations remain offline. In this work, we present a real-time tightly coupled GNSS-IMU FGO method that enables causal state estimation via incremental optimization with fixed-lag marginalization, and we evaluate its performance in a highly urbanized GNSS-degraded environment using the UrbanNav dataset.

</details>


### [179] [MEM: Multi-Scale Embodied Memory for Vision Language Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03596)
*Marcel Torne,Karl Pertsch,Homer Walke,Kyle Vedder,Suraj Nair,Brian Ichter,Allen Z. Ren,Haohuan Wang,Jiaming Tang,Kyle Stachowicz,Karan Dhabalia,Michael Equi,Quan Vuong,Jost Tobias Springenberg,Sergey Levine,Chelsea Finn,Danny Driess*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种多尺度具身记忆（MEM）方法，结合视频短时记忆和文本长时记忆，以支持机器人在复杂多阶段任务中的长时间控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在复杂的现实世界多阶段任务中，机器人需要在多个粒度上表示过去的事件，包括捕捉抽象语义概念的长期记忆和处理遮挡等短期事件的短期记忆。

Method: MEM结合基于视频的短时记忆（通过视频编码器压缩）和基于文本的长时记忆，形成混合模态的长时记忆架构。

Result: MEM使机器人策略能够执行长达十五分钟的任务，如清理厨房或制作烤奶酪三明治，并能在上下文中智能调整操作策略。

Conclusion: 有效的长时程机器人控制记忆架构应融合多种模态以捕获不同层次的抽象信息，MEM为此提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Conventionally, memory in end-to-end robotic learning involves inputting a sequence of past observations into the learned policy. However, in complex multi-stage real-world tasks, the robot's memory must represent past events at multiple levels of granularity: from long-term memory that captures abstracted semantic concepts (e.g., a robot cooking dinner should remember which stages of the recipe are already done) to short-term memory that captures recent events and compensates for occlusions (e.g., a robot remembering the object it wants to pick up once its arm occludes it). In this work, our main insight is that an effective memory architecture for long-horizon robotic control should combine multiple modalities to capture these different levels of abstraction. We introduce Multi-Scale Embodied Memory (MEM), an approach for mixed-modal long-horizon memory in robot policies. MEM combines video-based short-horizon memory, compressed via a video encoder, with text-based long-horizon memory. Together, they enable robot policies to perform tasks that span up to fifteen minutes, like cleaning up a kitchen, or preparing a grilled cheese sandwich. Additionally, we find that memory enables MEM policies to intelligently adapt manipulation strategies in-context.

</details>


### [180] [Touch2Insert: Zero-Shot Peg Insertion by Touching Intersections of Peg and Hole](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03627)
*Masaru Yajima,Yuma Shin,Rei Kawakami,Asako Kanezaki,Kei Ota*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Touch2Insert是一种基于触觉的零样本任意销钉插入框架，通过高分辨率触觉图像重建截面几何形状并估计孔与销钉的相对位姿，实现在单次接触下完成对齐与插入，无需任务特定训练，在仿真和真实机器人实验中均表现出高精度与强鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业连接器插入需要亚毫米级精度，但常面临视觉遮挡和未知几何形状的挑战。基于视觉的方法受限于遮挡和泛化能力，学习型策略难以迁移到新形状。因此需要一种不依赖视觉、能适应未见几何结构的可靠方法。

Method: 利用触觉传感捕捉接触点的局部表面几何信息，提出Touch2Insert框架：从高分辨率触觉图像中重建横截面几何形状，并以零样本方式估计孔相对于销钉的位姿；通过形状配准实现对齐，仅需一次接触即可完成插入，无需任务特定训练。

Result: 在三种不同连接器上进行了仿真和真实机器人实验。仿真中所有连接器的位姿估计精度达到亚毫米级；真实机器人上的平均成功率为86.7%。

Conclusion: 触觉感知能够有效克服视觉遮挡和几何泛化难题，Touch2Insert具备良好的鲁棒性和通用性，验证了触觉在实际机器人连接器插入任务中的潜力。

Abstract: Reliable insertion of industrial connectors remains a central challenge in robotics, requiring sub-millimeter precision under uncertainty and often without full visual access. Vision-based approaches struggle with occlusion and limited generalization, while learning-based policies frequently fail to transfer to unseen geometries. To address these limitations, we leverage tactile sensing, which captures local surface geometry at the point of contact and thus provides reliable information even under occlusion and across novel connector shapes. Building on this capability, we present \emph{Touch2Insert}, a tactile-based framework for arbitrary peg insertion. Our method reconstructs cross-sectional geometry from high-resolution tactile images and estimates the relative pose of the hole with respect to the peg in a zero-shot manner. By aligning reconstructed shapes through registration, the framework enables insertion from a single contact without task-specific training. To evaluate its performance, we conducted experiments with three diverse connectors in both simulation and real-robot settings. The results indicate that Touch2Insert achieved sub-millimeter pose estimation accuracy for all connectors in simulation, and attained an average success rate of 86.7\% on the real robot, thereby confirming the robustness and generalizability of tactile sensing for real-world robotic connector insertion.

</details>


### [181] [TreeLoc++: Robust 6-DoF LiDAR Localization in Forests with a Compact Digital Forest Inventory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03695)
*Minwoo Jung,Dongjae Lee,Nived Chebrolu,Haedam Oh,Maurice Fallon,Ayoung Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TreeLoc++ 是一种基于数字森林清查（DFI）的全局定位框架，直接利用树木的几何属性进行高效、可靠的6自由度姿态估计，无需依赖密集的点云数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的定位方法大多依赖于存储和维护成本高昂的密集点云数据，而忽略了在现代林业中广泛使用的数字森林清查（DFI）。为了实现可持续森林管理中的长期、可扩展的定位需求，需要一种更轻量、鲁棒的定位方案。

Method: 提出 TreeLoc++，一种直接以 DFI 为输入的定位框架。该方法通过引入成对距离直方图编码局部树布局上下文，并结合胸径（DBH）过滤和偏航一致的内点选择来减少误匹配。进一步利用树木几何约束优化，联合估计滚转、俯仰和高度，提升姿态稳定性。

Result: 在来自四个国家、三个数据集的27个序列上验证，TreeLoc++ 实现了厘米级定位精度。使用仅250KB地图数据表示15次会话、7.98公里轨迹，并在两年时间跨度的数据上表现出强健的长期定位能力，优于依赖点云的手工设计和学习基线方法。

Conclusion: TreeLoc++ 验证了以 DFI 作为判别表示用于长期、可扩展机器人定位的可行性，显著降低了存储开销并提升了在结构模糊森林中的定位可靠性，推动了可持续林业中自动化系统的实际部署。

Abstract: Reliable localization is essential for sustainable forest management, as it allows robots or sensor systems to revisit and monitor the status of individual trees over long periods. In modern forestry, this management is structured around Digital Forest Inventories (DFIs), which encode stems using compact geometric attributes rather than raw data. Despite their central role, DFIs have been overlooked in localization research, and most methods still rely on dense gigabyte-sized point clouds that are costly to store and maintain. To improve upon this, we propose TreeLoc++, a global localization framework that operates directly on DFIs as a discriminative representation, eliminating the need to use the raw point clouds. TreeLoc++ reduces false matches in structurally ambiguous forests and improves the reliability of full 6-DoF pose estimation. It augments coarse retrieval with a pairwise distance histogram that encodes local tree-layout context, subsequently refining candidates via DBH-based filtering and yaw-consistent inlier selection to further reduce mismatches. Furthermore, a constrained optimization leveraging tree geometry jointly estimates roll, pitch, and height, enhancing pose stability and enabling accurate localization without reliance on dense 3D point cloud data. Evaluations on 27 sequences recorded in forests across three datasets and four countries show that TreeLoc++ achieves precise localization with centimeter-level accuracy. We further demonstrate robustness to long-term change by localizing data recorded in 2025 against inventories built from 2023 data, spanning a two-year interval. The system represents 15 sessions spanning 7.98 km of trajectories using only 250KB of map data and outperforms both hand-crafted and learning-based baselines that rely on point cloud maps. This demonstrates the scalability of TreeLoc++ for long-term deployment.

</details>


### [182] [UrbanHuRo: A Two-Layer Human-Robot Collaboration Framework for the Joint Optimization of Heterogeneous Urban Services](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03701)
*Tonmoy Dey,Lin Jiang,Zheng Dong,Guang Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了UrbanHuRo，一个用于异构城市服务联合优化的两层人机协作框架，通过众包配送与城市感知的实际案例展示了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多孤立地优化单一城市服务，忽略了不同服务之间的相互作用，导致资源利用效率不高。因此，需要一种能够协同优化多种城市服务的新方法。

Method: 提出了一种名为UrbanHuRo的双层人机协作框架，包含基于MapReduce的K-子模最大化模块用于高效派单，以及基于深度子模奖励的强化学习算法用于感知路径规划。

Result: 在真实世界食品配送平台数据集上的实验表明，UrbanHuRo平均提高了29.7%的感知覆盖率和39.2%的快递员收入，并显著减少了超时订单数量。

Conclusion: UrbanHuRo框架能有效实现异构城市服务的联合优化，在提升服务效率和资源利用率方面具有显著优势。

Abstract: In the vision of smart cities, technologies are being developed to enhance the efficiency of urban services and improve residents' quality of life. However, most existing research focuses on optimizing individual services in isolation, without adequately considering reciprocal interactions among heterogeneous urban services that could yield higher efficiency and improved resource utilization. For example, human couriers could collect traffic and air quality data along their delivery routes, while sensing robots could assist with on-demand delivery during peak hours, enhancing both sensing coverage and delivery efficiency. However, the joint optimization of different urban services is challenging due to potentially conflicting objectives and the need for real-time coordination in dynamic environments. In this paper, we propose UrbanHuRo, a two-layer human-robot collaboration framework for joint optimization of heterogeneous urban services, demonstrated through crowdsourced delivery and urban sensing. UrbanHuRo includes two key designs: (i) a scalable distributed MapReduce-based K-submodular maximization module for efficient order dispatch, and (ii) a deep submodular reward reinforcement learning algorithm for sensing route planning. Experimental evaluations on real-world datasets from a food delivery platform demonstrate that UrbanHuRo improves sensing coverage by 29.7% and courier income by 39.2% on average in most settings, while also significantly reducing the number of overdue orders.

</details>


### [183] [Large-Language-Model-Guided State Estimation for Partially Observable Task and Motion Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03704)
*Yoonwoo Kim,Raghav Arora,Roberto Martín-Martín,Peter Stone,Ben Abbatematteo,Yoonchang Sung*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为CoCo-TAMP的分层状态估计框架，利用大语言模型（LLM）引导的常识知识来改进部分可观测环境中的机器人任务与运动规划，显著减少了规划和执行时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在部分可观测环境中，机器人常因无法感知所有物体而面临规划困难，尤其是当出现任务无关物体时，传统规划器往往忽略这些信息，导致效率低下。因此，需要引入常识性知识以提升规划效率和鲁棒性。

Method: 提出CoCo-TAMP框架，结合两种常识知识：特定位置更可能发现某些物体，相似物体倾向于共存。通过大语言模型获取并整合这些知识，构建分层状态估计机制，动态调整对任务相关物体的信念分布，从而优化任务与运动规划。

Result: 实验结果显示，在仿真环境中平均减少62.7%的规划与执行时间，真实世界演示中减少72.6%，显著优于未引入常识知识的基线方法。

Conclusion: 利用大语言模型提取常识知识并融入状态估计，能有效提升机器人在复杂、部分可观测环境中的长期任务规划效率和实用性。

Abstract: Robot planning in partially observable environments, where not all objects are known or visible, is a challenging problem, as it requires reasoning under uncertainty through partially observable Markov decision processes. During the execution of a computed plan, a robot may unexpectedly observe task-irrelevant objects, which are typically ignored by naive planners. In this work, we propose incorporating two types of common-sense knowledge: (1) certain objects are more likely to be found in specific locations; and (2) similar objects are likely to be co-located, while dissimilar objects are less likely to be found together. Manually engineering such knowledge is complex, so we explore leveraging the powerful common-sense reasoning capabilities of large language models (LLMs). Our planning and execution framework, CoCo-TAMP, introduces a hierarchical state estimation that uses LLM-guided information to shape the belief over task-relevant objects, enabling efficient solutions to long-horizon task and motion planning problems. In experiments, CoCo-TAMP achieves an average reduction of 62.7 in planning and execution time in simulation, and 72.6 in real-world demonstrations, compared to a baseline that does not incorporate either type of common-sense knowledge.

</details>


### [184] [X-Loco: Towards Generalist Humanoid Locomotion Control via Synergetic Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03733)
*Dewei Wang,Xinmiao Wang,Chenyun Zhang,Jiyuan Shi,Yingnan Zhao,Chenjia Bai,Xuelong Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: X-Loco是一个基于视觉的通用人形机器人运动框架，通过协同策略蒸馏和自适应专家选择机制，首次实现了将直立行走、全身协调和跌倒恢复等技能统一于单一策略中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法难以将多种人形机器人运动技能（如直立行走、跌倒恢复、全身协调）集成到一个统一策略中，因存在动力学差异和控制目标冲突。

Method: 提出X-Loco框架，训练多个专家策略，并通过协同策略蒸馏与案例自适应的专家选择机制，动态引导一个基于视觉的学生策略学习多技能。

Result: 实验表明，X-Loco在跌倒恢复、地形穿越等任务中表现优异，且无需参考动作，仅依赖速度指令即可实现多种运动技能的集成。

Conclusion: X-Loco是首个实现基于视觉、无需参考动作、集成多种复杂运动技能的人形机器人通用运动策略框架，显著提升了学习效率与技能广度。

Abstract: While recent advances have demonstrated strong performance in individual humanoid skills such as upright locomotion, fall recovery and whole-body coordination, learning a single policy that masters all these skills remains challenging due to the diverse dynamics and conflicting control objectives involved. To address this, we introduce X-Loco, a framework for training a vision-based generalist humanoid locomotion policy. X-Loco trains multiple oracle specialist policies and adopts a synergetic policy distillation with a case-adaptive specialist selection mechanism, which dynamically leverages multiple specialist policies to guide a vision-based student policy. This design enables the student to acquire a broad spectrum of locomotion skills, ranging from fall recovery to terrain traversal and whole-body coordination skills. To the best of our knowledge, X-Loco is the first framework to demonstrate vision-based humanoid locomotion that jointly integrates upright locomotion, whole-body coordination and fall recovery, while operating solely under velocity commands without relying on reference motions. Experimental results show that X-Loco achieves superior performance, demonstrated by tasks such as fall recovery and terrain traversal. Ablation studies further highlight that our framework effectively leverages specialist expertise and enhances learning efficiency.

</details>


### [185] [Characterization and Correlation of Robotic Snake Scale Friction and Locomotion Speed](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03735)
*Umit Sen,Andri Mahegan,Gina Olson*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新型模块化伪皮肤设计，用于软体蛇形机器人，并实验测量了不同攻角和表面下的摩擦特性与运动速度，发现摩擦比与运动速度之间缺乏一致的相关性，表明仅靠摩擦比可能不足以预测蛇形机器人的运动性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了提升软体蛇形机器人在多种环境中移动的能力，需要深入理解工程系统中摩擦各向异性的构建方式及其对运动速度和方向的影响。

Method: 设计了一种可拆卸鳞片的模块化伪皮肤，实验测量了不同攻角（15°, 25°, 35°, 45°）在不同表面（草地、树皮、光滑面、地毯）上的前后及侧向摩擦特性，并分别测量了每种情况下的运动速度。

Result: 观察到摩擦系数与鳞片攻角之间存在符合文献和直觉的趋势，但在不同表面和角度下，摩擦比与运动速度之间未表现出一致的相关性。

Conclusion: 仅依靠摩擦比可能不足以预测蛇形机器人的运动速度，或者需要特定的测量方法来准确获取这些摩擦比。

Abstract: Snake robots are inspired by the ability of biological snakes to move over rock, grass, leaves, soil, up trees, along pavement and more. Their ability to move in multiple distinct environments is due to their legless locomotion strategy, which combines distinct gaits with a skin that exhibits frictional anisotropy. Designing soft robotic snakes with similar capabilities requires an understanding of how this underlying frictional anisotropy should be created in engineered systems, and how variances in the frictional anisotropy ratio affect locomotion speed and direction on different surfaces. While forward and backward frictional ratios have been characterized for previous scale designs, lateral friction and the associated ratios are often overlooked. In this paper, our contributions include: (i) the development of a novel articulated pseudo-skin design that is modular, easy to construct and has removable or replaceable scales; (ii) experimental measurement of the frictional characteristics of otherwise-identical scales at varying angles of attack (15°, 25°, 35°, 45°) on different surfaces of interest (grass, bark, smooth surface, carpet);(iii) separate measurements of locomotion speed for each angle and surface. Consequently, while we observed some consistent trends between frictional coefficients and scale angle, aligning with literature and intuition, we were not able to consistently identify expected correlations between frictional ratios and locomotion speed. We conclude that either frictional ratios alone are not sufficient to predict the observed speed of a snake robot, or that specific measurement approaches are required to accurately capture these ratios.

</details>


### [186] [Whole-Body Safe Control of Robotic Systems with Koopman Neural Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03740)
*Sebin Jung,Abulikemu Abuduweili,Jiaxing Li,Changliu Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于Koopman算子的数据驱动框架，将非线性系统映射到高维线性空间，并结合安全集算法（SSA），在单个二次规划中实现轨迹跟踪与安全约束，验证于机械臂和四足机器人上表现出良好性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对强非线性、高维动力学系统实时控制中非线性优化与安全约束难以同时满足的问题，寻求一种可实时求解且保证安全与性能的控制方法。

Method: 采用数据驱动方式学习Koopman嵌入与算子，将非线性动态线性化表示于提升空间，并将其与安全集算法（SSA）结合，在统一的二次规划（QP）框架中同时处理跟踪与安全约束。

Result: 在Kinova Gen3机械臂和Go2四足机器人上的实验表明，该方法能实现精确的轨迹跟踪和有效避障，且控制可行性和最优性得到保障。

Conclusion: 所提方法通过Koopman线性化与SSA结合，实现了复杂非线性系统下的实时安全控制，无需额外安全滤波器，具有良好的应用潜力。

Abstract: Controlling robots with strongly nonlinear, high-dimensional dynamics remains challenging, as direct nonlinear optimization with safety constraints is often intractable in real time. The Koopman operator offers a way to represent nonlinear systems linearly in a lifted space, enabling the use of efficient linear control. We propose a data-driven framework that learns a Koopman embedding and operator from data, and integrates the resulting linear model with the Safe Set Algorithm (SSA). This allows the tracking and safety constraints to be solved in a single quadratic program (QP), ensuring feasibility and optimality without a separate safety filter. We validate the method on a Kinova Gen3 manipulator and a Go2 quadruped, showing accurate tracking and obstacle avoidance.

</details>


### [187] [HALyPO: Heterogeneous-Agent Lyapunov Policy Optimization for Human-Robot Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03741)
*Hao Zhang,Yaru Niu,Yikai Wang,Ding Zhao,H. Eric Tseng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为HALyPO的异构智能体李雅普诺夫策略优化方法，用于解决人机协作中因机器人与人类理性差异导致的学习不稳定性问题，通过在策略参数空间中引入李雅普诺夫函数保证去中心化学习的稳定性，从而提升泛化能力和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了提升人机协作中的泛化性和鲁棒性，需要应对人类行为和情境的组合多样性，但机器人与人类之间的异质性导致了学习过程中的理性差距（RG），使得传统的多智能体强化学习方法容易出现振荡或发散。

Method: 提出HALyPO方法，通过在策略参数空间中构建一个基于李雅普诺夫函数的每步下降条件，并采用最优二次投影修正去中心化的策略梯度，以确保理性差距的单调收缩，从而稳定学习过程。

Result: 在多个仿真环境和真实人形机器人实验中验证了HALyPO的有效性，结果显示该方法能够显著提高在协作边缘情况下的泛化能力和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: HALyPO通过将李雅普诺夫稳定性直接嵌入策略参数空间，有效解决了异构智能体协作中的学习不稳定问题，为开放-ended人机交互提供了可靠的去中心化学习框架。

Abstract: To improve generalization and resilience in human-robot collaboration (HRC), robots must handle the combinatorial diversity of human behaviors and contexts, motivating multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL). However, inherent heterogeneity between robots and humans creates a rationality gap (RG) in the learning process-a variational mismatch between decentralized best-response dynamics and centralized cooperative ascent. The resulting learning problem is a general-sum differentiable game, so independent policy-gradient updates can oscillate or diverge without added structure. We propose heterogeneous-agent Lyapunov policy optimization (HALyPO), which establishes formal stability directly in the policy-parameter space by enforcing a per-step Lyapunov decrease condition on a parameter-space disagreement metric. Unlike Lyapunov-based safe RL, which targets state/trajectory constraints in constrained Markov decision processes, HALyPO uses Lyapunov certification to stabilize decentralized policy learning. HALyPO rectifies decentralized gradients via optimal quadratic projections, ensuring monotonic contraction of RG and enabling effective exploration of open-ended interaction spaces. Extensive simulations and real-world humanoid-robot experiments show that this certified stability improves generalization and robustness in collaborative corner cases.

</details>


### [188] [Interaction-Aware Whole-Body Control for Compliant Object Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03751)
*Hao Zhang,Yves Tseng,Ding Zhao,H. Eric Tseng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种生物启发的交互导向全身控制（IO-WBC）方法，用于在非结构化环境中实现辅助人形机器人稳定协作物体运输。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的以跟踪为中心的全身控制在强交互力下不可靠，尤其在紧密接触支持任务中表现不佳。

Method: 将上半身交互执行与下半身支撑控制分离，结合轨迹优化参考生成器和基于强化学习的策略，并通过非对称师生蒸馏部署策略。

Result: 在模拟和实际实验中验证了该方法能在高负载和扰动下保持稳定的全身行为和物理交互。

Conclusion: IO-WBC能有效提升人形机器人在复杂场景下的协作搬运能力，即使在速度跟踪不可行时仍可实现顺应性物体运输。

Abstract: Cooperative object transport in unstructured environments remains challenging for assistive humanoids because strong, time-varying interaction forces can make tracking-centric whole-body control unreliable, especially in close-contact support tasks. This paper proposes a bio-inspired, interaction-oriented whole-body control (IO-WBC) that functions as an artificial cerebellum - an adaptive motor agent that translates upstream (skill-level) commands into stable, physically consistent whole-body behavior under contact. This work structurally separates upper-body interaction execution from lower-body support control, enabling the robot to maintain balance while shaping force exchange in a tightly coupled robot-object system. A trajectory-optimized reference generator (RG) provides a kinematic prior, while a reinforcement learning (RL) policy governs body responses under heavy-load interactions and disturbances. The policy is trained in simulation with randomized payload mass/inertia and external perturbations, and deployed via asymmetric teacher-student distillation so that the student relies only on proprioceptive histories at runtime. Extensive experiments demonstrate that IO-WBC maintains stable whole-body behavior and physical interaction even when precise velocity tracking becomes infeasible, enabling compliant object transport across a wide range of scenarios.

</details>


### [189] [Cognition to Control - Multi-Agent Learning for Human-Humanoid Collaborative Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03768)
*Hao Zhang,Ding Zhao,H. Eric Tseng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为C2C的三层架构，用于实现高效的人机协作，通过将高级意图转化为接触稳定的全身运动，并在多智能体环境中实现长期协调与实时控制的结合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉-语言-动作系统多关注反应式行为，缺乏对持续性、深思熟虑型（System 2）决策与低延迟连续控制融合机制的研究，尤其在需要长期协调和物理交互的多智能体人机协作中存在明显不足。

Method: 提出C2C三层架构：第一层基于VLM进行场景理解与具身化约束推理；第二层为基于去中心化MARL的规划层，建模为马尔可夫势博弈以优化技能序列；第三层为全身控制层，高频执行技能并保证运动可行性与接触稳定。第二层采用残差策略形式作用于基线控制器之上。

Result: 在协作操作任务中，相比单智能体和端到端基线方法表现出更高的成功率与鲁棒性，实现了稳定的协调行为和涌现的领导-跟随动态。

Conclusion: C2C架构有效弥合了高层认知推理与低层实时控制之间的鸿沟，支持在复杂人机协作任务中实现持久、稳定且适应性强的多智能体协同。

Abstract: Effective human-robot collaboration (HRC) requires translating high-level intent into contact-stable whole-body motion while continuously adapting to a human partner. Many vision-language-action (VLA) systems learn end-to-end mappings from observations and instructions to actions, but they often emphasize reactive (System 1-like) behavior and leave under-specified how sustained System 2-style deliberation can be integrated with reliable, low-latency continuous control. This gap is acute in multi-agent HRC, where long-horizon coordination decisions and physical execution must co-evolve under contact, feasibility, and safety constraints. We address this limitation with cognition-to-control (C2C), a three-layer hierarchy that makes the deliberation-to-control pathway explicit: (i) a VLM-based grounding layer that maintains persistent scene referents and infers embodiment-aware affordances/constraints; (ii) a deliberative skill/coordination layer-the System 2 core-that optimizes long-horizon skill choices and sequences under human-robot coupling via decentralized MARL cast as a Markov potential game with a shared potential encoding task progress; and (iii) a whole-body control layer that executes the selected skills at high frequency while enforcing kinematic/dynamic feasibility and contact stability. The deliberative layer is realized as a residual policy relative to a nominal controller, internalizing partner dynamics without explicit role assignment. Experiments on collaborative manipulation tasks show higher success and robustness than single-agent and end-to-end baselines, with stable coordination and emergent leader-follower behaviors.

</details>


### [190] [Learning Surgical Robotic Manipulation with 3D Spatial Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03798)
*Yu Sheng,Lidian Wang,Xiaomeng Chu,Jiajun Deng,Min Cheng,Yanyong Zhang,Bei Hua,Houqiang Li,Jianmin Ji*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为Spatial Surgical Transformer (SST)的端到端视觉运动策略，通过直接挖掘内窥镜图像中的3D空间线索，赋予手术机器人3D空间感知能力，并构建了大规模逼真的Surgical3D数据集以支持该方法，实验证明其在复杂手术任务中达到先进性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的手术机器人3D感知方法存在误差累积、无法端到端优化或因附加设备影响机械臂运动等问题，限制了其在临床中的实际应用，因此需要一种不依赖额外硬件且能实现高精度3D理解的解决方案。

Method: 提出SST框架：首先构建包含30K立体内窥镜图像对的大规模数据集Surgical3D；然后微调强大的几何Transformer从立体图像中提取鲁棒的3D潜在表示；最后通过轻量化的多级空间特征连接器（MSFC）在内窥镜中心坐标系下将3D表示与机器人动作空间对齐，实现端到端学习。

Result: 在真实机器人实验中，SST在打结和离体器官解剖等复杂手术任务上表现出最先进的性能和强空间泛化能力，优于现有方法。

Conclusion: SST为手术机器人提供了一种高效、实用的3D空间感知方案，推动了其在临床环境中的部署前景，同时发布的Surgical3D数据集为后续研究提供了重要资源。

Abstract: Achieving 3D spatial awareness is crucial for surgical robotic manipulation, where precise and delicate operations are required. Existing methods either explicitly reconstruct the surgical scene prior to manipulation, or enhance multi-view features by adding wrist-mounted cameras to supplement the default stereo endoscopes. However, both paradigms suffer from notable limitations: the former easily leads to error accumulation and prevents end-to-end optimization due to its multi-stage nature, while the latter is rarely adopted in clinical practice since wrist-mounted cameras can interfere with the motion of surgical robot arms. In this work, we introduce the Spatial Surgical Transformer (SST), an end-to-end visuomotor policy that empowers surgical robots with 3D spatial awareness by directly exploring 3D spatial cues embedded in endoscopic images. First, we build Surgical3D, a large-scale photorealistic dataset containing 30K stereo endoscopic image pairs with accurate 3D geometry, addressing the scarcity of 3D data in surgical scenes. Based on Surgical3D, we finetune a powerful geometric transformer to extract robust 3D latent representations from stereo endoscopes images. These representations are then seamlessly aligned with the robot's action space via a lightweight multi-level spatial feature connector (MSFC), all within an endoscope-centric coordinate frame. Extensive real-robot experiments demonstrate that SST achieves state-of-the-art performance and strong spatial generalization on complex surgical tasks such as knot tying and ex-vivo organ dissection, representing a significant step toward practical clinical deployment. The dataset and code will be released.

</details>


### [191] [SkillVLA: Tackling Combinatorial Diversity in Dual-Arm Manipulation via Skill Reuse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03836)
*Xuanran Zhai,Zekai Huang,Longyan Wu,Qianyou Zhao,Qiaojun Yu,Jieji Ren,Ce Hao,Harold Soh*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SkillVLA是一种新型双臂操作框架，通过显式支持技能重用，提升视觉-语言-动作模型在组合多样性任务中的表现，显著提高任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有双臂视觉-语言-动作模型未充分考虑单臂技能组合带来的组合多样性挑战，导致无法有效复用已有技能，限制了模型的可扩展性。

Method: 提出SkillVLA框架，解耦双臂技能表示，显式建模单臂技能的可重组性，支持跨新颖左右配对的技能重用，避免为每种组合单独学习。

Result: 实验表明，SkillVLA将任务成功率从0%提升至51%，在协作性和长视野任务中表现出色。

Conclusion: SkillVLA通过支持技能重用，有效解决了双臂操作中的组合多样性问题，提升了模型的泛化能力与可扩展性。

Abstract: Recent progress in vision-language-action (VLA) models has demonstrated strong potential for dual-arm manipulation, enabling complex behaviors and generalization to unseen environments. However, mainstream bimanual VLA formulations largely overlook the critical challenge of combinatorial diversity. Different pairings of single-arm behaviors can induce qualitatively distinct task behaviors, yet existing models do not explicitly account for this structure. We argue that effective bimanual VLAs should support skill reuse - the ability to recombine previously learned single-arm skills across novel left-right pairings - thereby avoiding the need to separately learn every possible combination. Current VLA designs entangle skills across arms, preventing such recomposition and limiting scalability. To address this limitation, we propose SkillVLA, a framework explicitly designed to enable skill reuse in dual-arm manipulation. Extensive experiments demonstrate that SkillVLA substantially improves skill composition, increasing overall success rate from 0% to 51%, and achieves strong performance on cooperative and long-horizon tasks.

</details>


### [192] [IROSA: Interactive Robot Skill Adaptation using Natural Language](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03897)
*Markus Knauer,Samuel Bustamante,Thomas Eiband,Alin Albu-Schäffer,Freek Stulp,João Silvério*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于工具架构的框架，利用预训练大语言模型实现机器人技能的开放词汇适应，无需微调或直接模型-硬件交互，在工业轴承环插入任务中验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将基础模型与模仿学习结合应用于机器人技能适应的研究较少，尤其是在工业部署方面缺乏关注，因此需要一种安全、可解释的方法来实现自然语言驱动的技能调整。

Method: 采用基于工具的架构，利用预训练大语言模型选择和参数化特定工具，通过抽象层隔离语言模型与机器人硬件，实现对机器人技能的调整。

Result: 在7自由度力矩控制机器人上成功实现了工业轴承环插入任务中的速度调节、轨迹修正和避障等自然语言指令驱动的技能适应。

Conclusion: 该框架能够在不进行微调的情况下实现开放词汇的机器人技能适应，同时保证安全性、透明性和可解释性，适合工业应用。

Abstract: Foundation models have demonstrated impressive capabilities across diverse domains, while imitation learning provides principled methods for robot skill adaptation from limited data. Combining these approaches holds significant promise for direct application to robotics, yet this combination has received limited attention, particularly for industrial deployment.
